Accepted proofs have passed the isolated workflow gates; an author-run comparator pass alone is not acceptance. Parents compose accepted dependencies in isolated worktrees. Only the root integrates and merges into the target. Child PRs are review records, closed unmerged after confirmed root delivery. PR states reflect their last recorded check.

The proof map

Workflow dependency DAG

Prerequisite → dependent. Select a theorem to explore its statement and evidence.

AcceptedMerged rootObserved workingNeeds attentionPending / activity unobserved208 theorems · 318 dependencies

A → B means B uses A. Children from one split are created together; their later stages proceed independently. Saved stages are checkpoints, not evidence of current execution. “Needs” lists prerequisite proofs or reusable acceptance evidence still pending. Historical acceptance is retained during evidence migration. The final prose proof identifies the lemmas actually used. Verification is distinct from a GitHub merge.
